Yeah… I guess I do. I mean, I know everybody gets behind sometimes, so I don’t want to make it sound overly dramatic, but… I keep feeling like I’ve messed things up for everyone.

It turns into this feeling that I’ve ruined things and… that maybe I deserve whatever bad thing happens next. I know that probably sounds harsh. Is that the kind of thing you mean?\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed that you feel more tired or that you have less energy than you used to, even when you’re not doing much?\n\nPatient: I’m not totally sure. I mean, I do feel worn down some days, but I don’t know if it’s more than what anyone would feel, you know? I can still get through what I need to do most of the time.\n\nMaybe my energy isn’t great, but I haven’t really been tracking it. Is that… is that what you’re asking?\n\nDoctor: Have you ever felt so low or hopeless that you’ve thought about hurting yourself or that life isn’t worth living?\n\nPatient: I… I don’t have a plan to hurt myself or anything like that. I don’t want to scare anyone.\n\nBut, yeah… sometimes when it gets really low, I catch myself thinking it might be easier if I just didn’t wake up. Like then nobody would have to deal with me anymore. I know that sounds bad, I’m sorry. I haven’t made any plan, though.\n\nDoctor: Have you noticed any significant changes in your appetite or weight over the past year?\n\nPatient: Not really, I don’t think so.
